2a1(1): @##/ o /ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das la rgyal po rnams dang /
2a2(1): blon po rnams dang /phyug po rnams dang /pho brang 'khor gyi mi rnams dang /tshong dpon 
2a3(1): rnams dang /ded dpon rnams dang /lha rnams dang /klu rnams dang /gnod sbyin rnams dang /lha ma yin rnams 
2b1(1): dang /nam mkha' lding rnams dang /mi 'am ci rnams dang /lto phye chen po rnams kyis/pa kur bsti byas/bla mar byas/
2b2(1): ri mor byas/mchod par byas te/lha dang /klu dang /gnod snod dang /lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' lding dang /

2b4(1): po dang ldan pa/na bza' dang /zhal zas dang /gzims cha dang /gdan dang /bsnyung gsos dang /sman zong rnams rnyes
3a1(1): @#/ O /pa |nyan thos kyi dge 'dun dang bcas pa mnyan du yod pa na/rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal/khyim bdag 
3a2(1): mgon med zas sbyin gyi kun dga' ra ba na bzhugs so//de'i tshe mnyan du yod pa na/ded dpon zhig gnas pa des rigs 
3a3(1): mnyam pa las chung ma blangs nas/de de dang lhan cig tu rtse zhing dga' la/dga' mgur spyod do//de rtse zhing dga' la dga' 
3a4(1): mgur spyod pa las de'i blo skyes pa/bdag ji srid du lang 'tsho la bab pa de srid du ni nor spel bar bya'o//phyis rgas pa'i tshe 
3b1(1): ni bde bar longs spyod par bya'o//snyam mo//de nas ded dpon de g.yog 'khor tshong pa lnga brgya dang /rgya mtsho'i gru nyos nas/chung ma dang bcas te/mtsho
3b2(1): chen po'i nang du zhugs so//de nas re shig/de'i chung ma la bu chags nas/rgya mtsho de nyid kyi nang du bu byung ste/khyi'u gzugs bzang zhing 
3b3(1): blta na stug pa mdzes pa zhig btsas so//de nas de btsas pa'i btsas ston byas nas/ming brgya mtsho zhes btagaso//de nas re shig 
3b4(1): na ded dpon de gru'i don grub nas/nye zho med par rgya mtsho chen po nas phyir 'khor to//de nas gang gi tshe khye'u rgya mtsho cher skyes pa de'i 
3b5(1): tshe/phas ded dpon du bkos nas/g.yog 'khor tshong pa lnga brgyadang /rgya mtsho chen por btang ste/de mthar gyis song song ba las/grong 
4a1(1): @#/ O /dang ;grong rdal dang /grong khyer dang /yul 'khor dang /rgyal po'i pho brang dang tshong 'dus rnams mthong nas/rgya mtsho'i 'gram 
4a2(1): du phyin te/kar sha pa Ni lnga brgya gru'i gla byin nas/zho shas 'tsho ba rnam pa lnga ste/gru shol dang chu 'phyag pa dang /mnyan pa dang /byi bzung mkhan 
4a3(1): dang /chu skya 'dzin pa rnams kyang khrid de/des lan gsum du dril bsgrags nas rgya mtsho chen por zhugaso//de nas de dag rgya mtsho chen por srin
4a4(1): nag po'i rlung gis gru de phu mdar bskor to//ded dpon rgya mtsho ni mu stegs can la dga' ste/de dus ma yin par 'chi bas 'jigs skrag nas/ston 
4a5(1): pa drug la gsol ba 'debs pa la zhugs kyang /gru de rlung gis ye bskor to//de nas re shig na/tshong pa gzhan dag kyang /lha stong snyed la gsol 
4b1(1): ba 'debs pa la zhugs te smras pa/gu lang chu lha ku be ra dang /drang srong sna tshogs lha dang /sa la gnas pa'i klu mang po de dag la/bdag cag dad pa 
4b2(1): lags kyis bdag cag 'di ni 'chi ba'i gnod gyur las myur du sdug bsngal 'di dag bsal te/ma 'gron po rnams ni bskyab tu gsol//de nas de dag gis de 
4b3(1): ltar smre sngags 'don kyang bskyabs byed pa su yang med do//de nas re shig na/dge bsnyen zhig gru de'i nang na 'dug pa des smras pa/kye ston pa drug dang /lha gzhan 
4b4(1): dag gis kyang /khyed la ci yang byar med kyis/mngon sum du yod pa'i lha sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das la/snying nas skyabasu song shig dang /de khyed kyi skyabs su 
4b5(1): 'gyur ro zhes byas so//de nas ded dpon rgya mtsho la sogs pa tshod pa lnga brgya po rnams kyis skad bltas te/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasla skyabs su song ngo //
4b6(1): de'i skabs su sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams kyis ni mi mkhyen pa'am/mi gzigs pa'am/mi rtogs pa'am/thugs su mi chud pa ci yang med la/sang+sa 
5a1(1): @#/ O /rgyas bcom ldan 'das thugs rje chen po dang ldan pa/'jig rten la phan 'dogs pa la zhugs pa/srung ba gcig pa dang ldan pa/gcig
5a2(1): tu dpa' ba/zla med pa/gnyis su mi gsung ba/zhi gnas dang /lhag mthong la gnas pa/rig pa gsum snang bar mdzad pa/bslab pa gsum la legs par 
5a3(1): bslabs pa/'dul ba'i gzhi gsum la mkhas pa/chu bo bzhi las rgal ba/rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa bzhi'i zhabs kyi mthil shin tu brtan pa/bsdu ba'i 
5a4(1): dngos po bzhi la yun ring po nas 'dris (bar byas )pa/mi 'jigs pa bzhis mi 'jigs pa/khyu mchog gi yang dag pa'i seng ge'i sgra chen po bsgrogs pa/yan lag 
5a5(1): lnga rnam par spangs pa/'gro ba lnga las yang dag par 'das pa/yan lag drug dang ldan pa/pha rol phyin pa drug yongasu rdzogs pa/rtag tu gnas par bya ba 
5a6(1): drug la gnas pa/byang chub kyi yan lag bdun gyi me tog gis phyug pa lam yan lag brgyad pa ston pa/mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug 
5b1(1): pa dgu la mkhas pa/stobs bcu'i stobs dang ldan pa/phyogs bcur grags pas kun du rgyas pa/brgya phrag bcu la dbang byed pas kyang ches khyad du 'phags pa rnams
5b2(1): ni/chos nyid kyis nyin dus gsum mtshan dus gsum du sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyis 'jig rten la gzigs te/su ni rgud/su ni dar/su ni phongs shing 'dug/su ni
5b3(1): nyam (nga)bar gyur/su ni gnod pa dang ldan /su ni 'phongs pa dang /nyam nga ba dang /gnod pa dang ldan /su ningan song du gzhol/su ni ngan song du bab/su ni ngan song nas 
5b4(1): phyung ste/mtho ris dang /thar pa dang /'bras bu la bzhag par bya/su ni 'dod pa'i 'dam du bying ba las lag nas drang bar bya/su ni 'phags pa'i nor bdun med pa 
5b5(1): las 'phags pa'i nor bdun gyi dbang phyug la dbang byed du gzhug par bya/su ni yongs su smin pa mas ma skyes pa las bskyed par bya/su ni dge ba'i rtsa ba rnam(sa)
5b6(1): bskyed zin pa las yongasu smin par bya/su ni yongasu smin pa las rnam par grol bar bya snyam du ye shes gzigs pa 'dugo/'dir smras pa/rgya mtsho chu srin rnams 
5b7(1): kyi gnas//dus rlabs yol bar 'gyur yang srid//'dus bar bya ba'i sras rnams la//sangs rgyas dus las yol ba med//de nas re shig na/bcom ldan 'ad(sa)
6a1(1): @#//rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal na bzhugs pas/nyi ma stong bas lhag pa'i 'od zer gyi kha dog lta bu'i 'od bkye nas/de dag gis tshor ba de dag kun
6a2(1): snang bar byas so//bskal pa stong du yongs su sgoms pa'i byams 'od zer dag kyang bkye ste/de dag gis sim par byas nas/srin po nag po'i rlung yang bzlog go/de
6a3(1): nas re shig na/ded dpon rgya mtsho gru'i don grub nas/nye zho med par rgya mtsho chen po'i nang nas phyir 'khor te/'chi bas skyo bar gyur pa de nyid kyis/sbyin pa dang rab 
6a4(1): tu sbyin pa dag byin nas/nye du'i skye bo rnams dbugs phyung ste/dge sbyong dang /bram ze dang /bkren pa dang /sdug cing 'phongs pa rnams tshim par byas nas/tshong pa lnga 
6a5(1): brgya 'khor ril bcom ldan 'das kyi bstan pa la rab tu byung ste/des brtson pa dang /bsgrub pa dang /'bab pas phol mig lnga pa'i 'khor ba'i 'khor lo 'di nyid g.yo
6a6(1): zhing mi bstan par rig nas/'du byed kyi rnam pa thams cad snyil ba dang /ltung ba dang /rnam par 'thor ba dang /rnam par 'jig pa'i chos can yin par rtsad chod de/nyon 
6a7(1): mongs pa thams cad spangs nas/dgra bcom pa nyid mngon du byas te/dgra bcom pa khams gsum pa'i 'dod chags dang bral bar gyur nas/gser dang bong par 
6b1(1): mnyam pa/nam mkha' dang lag mthil du 'dra ba'i sems dang ldan pa/tsan d+han spangs pa lta bur bsil bar gyur pa/rim pa'i sgo nga'i sbubs bcom pa/rig 
6b2(1): pa dang /mngon par shes pa dang /so so yang dag par rig pa thob pa/srid pa'i rnyed pa dang /chags pa dang /bkur sti la mi lta ba/dbang po dang /nye(྾)dbang dang bcas pa'i 
6b3(1): lha rnams kyis mchod cing brjed pa dang /gus par smra ba'i gnas su gyur to//de nas dge slong dag the tshom skyes nas/the tshom thams cad gcod pa/sangs rgyas bco 
6b4(1): ldan 'das la zhus pa/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das kyis/rgya mtsho lasogs pa tshong pa lnga brgya po 'di dag srog phangs pa bsal te/'phongs pa las yongs su bskyab+sa 
6b5(1): pa dang /gzha' gsang gi mthar thug pa/mya ngan las 'das pa la bkod pa ni/ngo mtshar lagaso//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/dge slong dag da ltar nga 'dod 
6b6(1): chags dang bral ba dang /gti mug dang bral ba dang /skye ba dang /rga ba dang /na ba dang /'chi ba dang /mya ngan dang /smre sngags 'don pa dang /sdug bsngal ba dang /yid mi ba/de 
6b7(1): ba dang /'khrug pa rnams las yongasu 'gro ba thams cad mkhyen pa/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa/ye shes dang /shes bya thams cad la dbang thob zin pa'i tshe/ngas 
6b8(1): rgya mtsho lasogs pa tshong pa lda brgya po'i de dag srog phyangs pa byin te/'phongs pa las yongasu skyabs pa dang /gzha' gsang gi thar thug pa/mya ngan las (྾)'das pa 
7a1(1): @#/ o /la bkod pa la 'di skad lta la ngo mtshar che ba ci yod//'das pa'i dus na /nga 'dod chags dang bcas pa/zhe sdang dang bcas pa/gti mug dang bcas 
7a2(1): pa/skye ba dang /rga ba dang /na ba dang /'chi ba dang /mya ngan dang /sme bsngags 'don pa dang /sdug bsngal ba dang /yid mi bde ba dang /'khrug pa rnams las yongasu ma grol ba'i

7a6(1): wA ra NA si'i tshong pa lnga brgya rgya mtsho chen po'i nang du 'gro bdod par gyur te/de dag song mthar gyis song song nas/rgya mtsho'i 'gram du phyin pa dang /de dag gis drang srong de

7a8(1): zhig 'byung bar gyur na/bcom ldan 'das khyod kyis bdag cag yongasu bskyab par gsol/zhes byas nas des kyang gnang ste/de ltar bya'o zhes byas so//de nas tshong pa de

7b5(1): sangs rgyas su gyur pa de'i tshe/bdag cag kyang dgongs par gsol/drang srong gis smras pa/de ltar bya'o//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/dge slong dag ci 
7b6(1): snyam du sems/de'i tshe de'i dus na drang du gyur pa gang yin pa de ni nga yin no//tshong pa de dag ni rgya mtsho lasogs pa 'di dag yin te/de'i tshe yang dag 'di dag yongasu 
7b7(1): bskyangaso//gzhan yang bcom ldan 'das 'od srung gi ring la rab tu byung ste/der de dag gis dbang po yongasu smin par byas pa des na/da ltar dgra bcom pa nyid mngon

8a7(1): par byas te/lha dang /klu dang /gnod sbyin dang /lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' ldingad dang /mi 'am ci dang /lto 'phye chen po rnams kyis mchod cing shes pa'i bcom ldan 'das bsod

9b3(1): la song ste/ched 'ga' ni bum pa sngon du btang la//dge tshul ba sna ma phyi bzhin 'ong ngo //ched 'ga' ni dge tshul ba sna ma sngon du 'gro la bum pa phyi bzhin 'ong ngo de'i tshe/bcom
